## Implementation Points: Dependencies and Parallelization

### Mandatory Serial Main Chain

`P0 → P1 → P2 → P3 → P3.5 → P4 → P5 → P6 → (P7 ∥ P8) → P9 → P10`

> **P3 emergency stopgap path (decision of 2026-10-05)**: P3’s prerequisites are narrowed to
> **2.3.2 + 2.4.1 + 2.4.3** (multi-file corpus tier + two red criteria). The rest of P2 (2.1 IR
> verifier, 2.2 snapshots, 2.3.1/2.3.3 differential testing and performance baseline) may proceed in
> parallel with P3. Stopping correctness vulnerabilities must not be blocked by
> criterion-infrastructure construction; however, **P4 must still wait for all of P2**—the unified
> Driver requires all three criterion tiers to be in place.

| Constraint                | Reason                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| P0 → P1                   | The three prohibitions constrain every P1 action (1.2.x adds `mod` declarations to `mod.rs`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| P1 → P2                   | Restoring tests changes the test count and corpus baseline                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| P2 (after narrowing) → P3 | Vulnerability criteria must **first be written in red** (2.4.1/2.4.3) and the multi-file corpus tier (2.3.2) must exist so that red tests have somewhere to run                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| P3 → P4                   | Fix bugs before refactoring; reversing the order causes bugs to be固化 into the phase table as “established behavior”                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| P3.5 → P4                 | RFC-029g’s (#399) deletion surface covers P4 4.2.2 / P5 / P6 / P7 / P8’s respective rewriting targets (the inventory is in §P3.5 of this document)—reversing the order means redoing the same deletions on the new grammar, new checker layout, and new IR (D56)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| P4 → P5                   | The `include!` refactoring and checker splitting modify the same file                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| P5 → P6                   | Continuous changes to the same file must be separated; otherwise regressions cannot be bisected                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| P6 → P7 / P8              | If type representations do not converge first, the new IR grows into a third representation; **P7’s gate = the A/B/C exits of 6.9** (I1–I3, I5, and I7; namely 6.9.2 including C3, 6.9.3 including d, and completion of 6.9.4), while D (Σ) does not block; P7 may consume only quantifier-derived views and the defeq interface and must not establish a parallel ledger; the 7.0 pure-move batch is a prerequisite for P7, is not constrained by this gate, and must not consume the undecided defeq/quantifier interfaces |
| P7/P8 → P9                | 9.5’s snapshot baseline and 9.3’s `pub(crate)` leak count reach their final form only after P7/P8                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

### Parallelizable Groups

| Group                                        | Members                                                                            | Basis                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| -------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **A (the only group authorized by RFC-039)** | P7 ∥ P8                                                                            | The file sets are disjoint: `ir.rs`/`ir_gen.rs`/`bytecode.rs`/`translator.rs` vs `lexer/*`/`parser/*`                                                                                                                                                       |
| B                                            | 1.2.1 ∥ 1.2.2 ∥ 1.2.3 ∥ 1.2.4                                                      | Each independently adds a `mod`; revert file by file                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| C                                            | 2.1 ∥ 2.2 ∥ 2.3.1 ∥ 2.3.2 ∥ 2.4                                                    | The three criterion tiers are mutually independent                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| D                                            | 4.4 (proof-layer cleanup) ∥ the entire P6 track                                    | `02` explicitly states in S5 that it “should proceed independently of S1–S4”                                                                                                                                                                                |
| E                                            | 4.2.5 ∥ 4.2.6 ∥ 4.2.8                                                              | All become mutually independent after 4.1                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| F                                            | 9.3 ∥ 9.9 ∥ 9.10 (the three scripts introduced in P0 may be developed in parallel) | 9.5’s snapshot baseline must be taken last                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| G                                            | S3 ∥ S4 ∥ S5                                                                       | The file sets are disjoint (verified on 2026-10-05: S3 modifies `docs/src/dev/design/check/` + `config.js`; S4 modifies `docs/src/rfc/` + the `check_tracking.py` generator; S5 modifies 2 `pub` items in `src/frontend/core/typecheck/`; no pair overlaps) |

**Cannot run in parallel (file overlap)**: P1(1.1.x) ∥ P8(8.0.x); 1.2.1 ∥ 8.3.2; 6.0 ∥ S2 (B6/E5 in
the same file batch); 4.3.2 ∥ 9.2 (now unified under P4); **3.4 ∥ P3.5** (`checker.rs` /
`environment.rs` are the same files—the external track’s delete-pub batch and the silent-channel
fixes must be serial).

**New constraints within the document**: 6.0→6.1→6.2→6.3→6.4→6.5→6.6 is strictly serial; 7.0 →
7a→7b→7c→7d is strictly serial (7.0 is the pure-move prerequisite batch for P7), and 7b’s `Phi` arm
must precede 7d.

## Key Decisions and Rationale

- **P0 is independent rather than merged into P9**—gates are “post-hoc checks,” while rules are
  “prior judgment.” P9’s scripts cannot prevent “a patch was applied when a refactoring was
  required”; only D0–D4’s review checklist can.
- **P9 is the sole authoritative inventory of CI scripts**—the three coexisting inventories from gap
  G3 made closure impossible. After consolidation, 10 active scripts each belong to one phase (9.8
  was cancelled by the user on 2026-11-24), and report-only status and hard-failure timing are
  explicitly traceable.
- **P3.5 incorporates RFC-029g rather than leaving it as an “independent track to do after the
  refactoring”**—its deletion surface overlaps the rewriting surfaces of P4/P5/P6/P7/P8 (RFC-039
  D56). Deferring it until after the refactoring means redoing the same deletions on the new
  grammar, new checker layout, and new IR; this is the same lesson as incorporating `2315`/`2341`
  into 7e and RFC status corrections into S4.
- **P5’s steps are supplied by this document rather than merged into P4**—P4 is already the largest
  single-point risk, and adding checker splitting would exceed the revertible granularity.
- **5.3.2 uses “zero caller changes” as its hard acceptance criterion**—the split must be
  independently revertible.

## Known Limitations and Risks

- **1.3.x (fixing exposed defects) has no regression criterion**—it only says “the fixed defects
  should not be rolled back” and lacks a requirement to record “what was fixed.” **Execution
  convention**: the commit message for every 1.3.x fix must reference the fixed test name and defect
  description as evidence for subsequent bisection.
- ~~**7b.1 is too coarse a granularity**~~ **Fixed (2026-10-05)**: split into 7b.1a / 7b.1b as two
  exclusive commits per DoD 6.
- **8.2.1 is too coarse a granularity**—approximately 89 reference renames, and a missed change
  **silently changes associativity**.
- ~~**D17 / D20 have no corresponding Level 3 tasks**~~ **Fixed (2026-10-05)**: D17 landed as 7e.1,
  and D20 landed as 4.5.1/4.5.2; D32/D34’s opcode reclamation was incorporated into 7e.1, with the
  deletion action left in P10 S6.
- ~~**The `2315` / `2341` data-loss defects were originally planned as “separate issues”**~~ **Fixed
  (2026-10-05)**: incorporated as 7e.2 / 7e.3. The exception table (throw/try) is core language
  semantics and must not be left as a future implementation.
- ~~**E2 (`lib.rs:568/582` `todo!()`) has no phase ownership**~~ **Fixed (2026-10-05)**:
  incorporated into P10 S2.
- ~~**F7’s opcode-gate extractor has ambiguous ownership**~~ **Fixed (2026-10-05)**: incorporated
  into the generation-time gate task in P10 S6.

## Conflict Registry: All Conflicts Adjudicated

> **The original “Conflict Registry” C1–C8 and all open questions in the individual documents have
> converged into the [RFC-039 decision registry](../../rfc/accepted/039-compiler-architecture.md)
> (D1–D50).** This document retains no pending items. The correspondence is as follows:

| Original number | Issue                                                                               | Decision                                                                                                                                                                       |
| --------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| C1              | `precedence_inline.rs` is restored in P1 and deleted in P8                          | **Restore it** (P1’s prerequisite is restoring tests with real assertions); P8 no longer deletes the file (Pratt is retained)                                                  |
| C2              | “Grammar-driven” commitment vs table-driven implementation                          | **Fallback solution**: retain Pratt + one declarative precedence table + mutual cross-validation of the two `BinOp` sources (the LALRPOP route is withdrawn), see P8 8.3       |
| C3              | Conflict between the C6 category definition and S1 classification                   | **S1 is classified under C6** (restoration does not require equivalence criteria, only regression tests); the C6 definition is expanded to “pure deletion or pure restoration” |
| C4              | `test_release_plan_spans_consumed` allowlist                                        | **No allowlist**; the difference set must be empty; if it cannot be empty, the `ReleasePlan` contract is defective → change to `PlanId` (D20/D41)                              |
| C5              | Internal differences between the two `Aggregation` functions have not been verified | **P4’s 4.2.7 lists this as a mandatory verification item**; confirm line by line during implementation                                                                         |
| C6              | The wasm playground fix requires P4’s `ProgramKind`                                 | **The wasm-path defect fix is incorporated into 4.2.8**; P3 handles only the three native entry points                                                                         |
| C7              | Ownership of `method_def_ordinals`                                                  | **Assigned to 04** (P7 batch c), see D21                                                                                                                                       |
| C8              | Correction of `upvalue_count` and the `.42` version number                          | **Fix it + raise `VERSION` from 4 to 5** (the format header already has `MAGIC` + `VERSION` fields, `codegen/bytecode.rs:14-16`), see D17                                      |
